A Freshman refers to a student who is in their first year of college or university, while an Infant refers to a person in the early stages of life, typically the first year of life. In this essay, we will explore the differences and similarities between these two concepts, focusing on their social, economic, and environmental implications.

II. Social Implications

A Freshman in college is often characterized by a sense of independence, experimentation, and self-discovery. They are leaving home for the first time and are seeking to form their own identity.
